{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nConformal Refinement Module (CRM).\\n\\nInput: base_action (30D, argmin-selected) + uncertainty_features (40D) + obs_features (960D from VLM)\\nOutput: refined_action (30D) = base_action + clipped_delta\\n\\nArchitecture: 2 hidden layers, dim 256, ReLU, residual connection.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\n\\n\\nclass ConformalRefinementModule(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Learn to refine VLA action predictions using structured uncertainty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, action_dim: int = 30, uncertainty_dim: int = 40,\\n                 obs_dim: int = 960, hidden_dim: int = 256,\\n                 max_correction_norm: float = 1.42):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Args:\\n            action_dim: flattened action dim (H_eff * D = 5 * 6 = 30)\\n            uncertainty_dim: CGS output dim (30 + 5 + 5 = 40), 0 for obs_only\\n            obs_dim: VLM encoder frozen output dim (960)\\n            hidden_dim: hidden layer dim\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        super().__init__()\\n\\n        self.max_correction_norm = max_correction_norm\\n        self.uncertainty_dim = uncertainty_dim\\n        input_dim = action_dim + uncertainty_dim + obs_dim\\n\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\n            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),\\n            nn.ReLU(),\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, hidden_dim),\\n            nn.ReLU(),\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, action_dim),\\n        )\\n\\n        # Residual: output = base_action + delta. Init last layer near zero.\\n        nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].weight)\\n        nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].bias)\\n\\n    def forward(self, base_action: torch.Tensor, uncertainty_features: torch.Tensor,\\n                obs_features: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Args:\\n            base_action: (B, 30) argmin-selected sample, flattened\\n            uncertainty_features: (B, 40) CGS output, or None for obs_only\\n            obs_features: (B, 960) VLM frozen obs features\\n        Returns:\\n            refined_action: (B, 30) corrected action\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if uncertainty_features is not None:\\n            x = torch.cat([base_action, uncertainty_features, obs_features], dim=-1)\\n        else:\\n            x = torch.cat([base_action, obs_features], dim=-1)\\n        delta = self.net(x)\\n        # L2 norm clipping to prevent catastrophic over-correction\\n        delta_norm = delta.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\\n        delta = delta * torch.clamp(self.max_correction_norm / (delta_norm + 1e-8), max=1.0)\\n        return base_action + delta\\n\\n    def count_parameters(self) -> int:\\n        return sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters() if p.requires_grad)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.18}}"}
```

(B, H, D)\\n            expert_flat = expert.reshape(expert.shape[0], -1)  # (B, 30)\\n\\n            # CGS (uncertainty features only — base action is argmin-selected, not mean)\\n            uf, _mean = cgs.compute_features_batch(k_samp)\\n            if scalar_unc:\\n                uf = _to_scalar_uf(uf)\\n            uf = uf.to(device)\\n            ba = _argmin_base_action(k_samp).to(device)  # (B, 30)\\n\\n            # CRM forward\\n            refined = crm(ba, None if obs_only else uf, obs_feat)\\n            loss = loss_fn(refined, expert_flat)\\n\\n            # Synthetic augmentation: perturb inputs, keep clean target\\n            n_aug = int(obs_feat.shape[0] * args.aug_ratio)\\n            if n_aug > 0:\\n                ba_noise = noise_scale.repeat(H_EFF)\\n                aug_ba = ba[:n_aug] + torch.randn_like(ba[:n_aug]) * ba_noise\\n                aug_uf = uf[:n_aug] + torch.randn_like(uf[:n_aug]) * noise_scale.mean() * 0.1\\n                aug_refined = crm(aug_ba, None if obs_only else aug_uf, obs_feat[:n_aug])\\n                aug_loss = loss_fn(aug_refined, expert_flat[:n_aug])\\n                loss = loss + 0.5 * aug_loss\\n\\n            optimizer.zero_grad()\\n            loss.backward()\\n            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(crm.parameters(), 1.0)\\n            optimizer.step()\\n\\n            epoch_loss += loss.item()\\n            n_batches += 1\\n\\n            if (batch_idx + 1) % args.log_every == 0:\\n                print(f\\\"  epoch {epoch} batch {batch_idx+1}/{len(train_loader)} \\\"\\n                      f\\\"loss={loss.item():.6f}\\\")\\n\\n        scheduler.step()\\n        avg_train_loss = epoch_loss / max(n_batches, 1)\\n\\n        # ── Validate ──\\n        crm.eval()\\n        val_loss = 0.0\\n        n_val_batches = 0\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            for batch in val_loader:\\n                obs_feat = batch[\\\"obs_features\\\"].to(device)\\n                k_samp = batch[\\\"k_samples\\\"].to(device)\\n                expert = batch[\\\"expert_action\\\"].to(device)\\n                expert_flat = expert.reshape(expert.shape[0], -1)\\n\\n                uf, _mean = cgs.compute_features_batch(k_samp)\\n                if scalar_unc:\\n                    uf = _to_scalar_uf(uf)\\n                uf = uf.to(device)\\n                ba = _argmin_base_action(k_samp).to(device)\\n\\n                refined = crm(ba, None if obs_only else uf, obs_feat)\\n                val_loss += loss_fn(refined, expert_flat).item()\\n                n_val_batches += 1\\n\\n        avg_val_loss = val_loss / max(n_val_batches, 1)\\n\\n        print(f\\\"Epoch {epoch}/{args.epochs}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"train_loss={avg_train_loss:.6f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"val_loss={avg_val_loss:.6f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"lr={scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e}\\\")\\n\\n        metrics = {\\\"train_loss\\\": avg_train_loss, \\\"val_loss\\\": avg_val_loss}\\n        ckpt_mgr.save(crm, optimizer, epoch, metrics)\\n\\n        if avg_val_loss < best_val_loss:\\n            best_val_loss = avg_val_loss\\n            torch.save(crm.state_dict(),\\n                       Path(args.output_dir) / \\\"crm_best.pt\\\")\\n            print(f\\\"  → New best val_loss: {best_val_loss:.6f}\\\")\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n[train] Done. Best val_loss={best_val_loss:.6f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"[train] Checkpoints at {args.output_dir}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    args = parse_args()\\n    print(f\\\"[train_crm] args: {vars(args)}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"[train_crm] device: {'cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu'}\\\")\\n\\n    if args.precompute:\\n        precompute_features(args)\\n        return\\n\\n    if args.auto:\\n        needs_precompute = any(\\n            not (Path(args.cache_dir) / bm / \\\"manifest.json\\\").exists()\\n            for bm in args.benchmark\\n        )\\n        if needs_precompute:\\n            print(\\\"[auto] Cache not found for some benchmarks, precomputing...\\\")\\n            precompute_features(args)\\n\\n    train(args)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

\\\"manifest.json\\\").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2))\\n    print(f\\\"[merge] Saved {obs_merged.shape[0]} samples + manifest to {cache_dir}\\\")\\n\\n\\n# ── Dataset ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\nclass CRMDataset(Dataset):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Dataset of precomputed (obs_features, k_samples, expert_action) tuples.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, obs_features, k_samples, expert_actions):\\n        self.obs_features = obs_features    # (N, 960)\\n        self.k_samples = k_samples          # (N, K, H_eff, CORRECTION_DIM)\\n        self.expert_actions = expert_actions  # (N, H_eff, CORRECTION_DIM)\\n\\n    def __len__(self):\\n        return self.obs_features.shape[0]\\n\\n    def __getitem__(self, idx):\\n        return {\\n            \\\"obs_features\\\": self.obs_features[idx],\\n            \\\"k_samples\\\": self.k_samples[idx],\\n            \\\"expert_action\\\": self.expert_actions[idx],\\n        }\\n\\n\\n# ── Training ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef _argmin_base_action(k_samp: \\\"torch.Tensor\\\") -> \\\"torch.Tensor\\\":\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Select the sample closest to the mean (argmin Euclidean) per batch element.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    B, K = k_samp.shape[0], k_samp.shape[1]\\n    k_flat = k_samp.reshape(B, K, -1)        # (B, K, 30)\\n    mean_a = k_flat.mean(dim=1, keepdim=True)  # (B, 1, 30)\\n    dists = ((k_flat - mean_a) ** 2).sum(dim=-1)  # (B, K)\\n    best_idx = dists.argmin(dim=1)             # (B,)\\n    return k_flat[torch.arange(B, device=k_flat.device), best_idx]  # (B, 30)\\n\\n\\ndef train(args):\\n    set_seed(args.seed)\\n    device = torch.device(\\\"cuda\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\"cpu\\\")\\n\\n    all_obs_features = []\\n    all_k_samples = []\\n    all_expert_actions = []\\n    manifest = None\\n\\n    for bm in args.benchmark:\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 3434 字符] ...\\n\\n obs_feat = batch[\\\"obs_features\\\"].to(device)       # (B, 960)\\n            k_samp = batch[\\\"k_samples\\\"].to(device)             # (B, K, H, D)\\n            expert = batch[\\\"expert_action\\\"].to(device)         # (B, H, D)\\n            expert_flat = expert.reshape(expert.shape[0], -1)  # (B, 30)\\n\\n            # CGS (uncertainty features only — base action is argmin-selected, not mean)\\n            uf, _mean = cgs.compute_features_batch(k_samp)\\n            if scalar_unc:\\n                uf = _to_scalar_uf(uf)\\n            uf = uf.to(device)\\n            ba = _argmin_base_action(k_samp).to(device)  # (B, 30)\\n\\n            # CRM forward\\n            refined = crm(ba, None if obs_only else uf, obs_feat)\\n            loss = loss_fn(refined, expert_flat)\\n\\n            # Synthetic augmentation: perturb inputs, keep clean target\\n            n_aug = int(obs_feat.shape[0] * args.aug_ratio)\\n            if n_aug > 0:\\n                ba_noise = noise_scale.repeat(H_EFF)\\n                aug_ba = ba[:n_aug] + torch.randn_like(ba[:n_aug]) * ba_noise\\n                aug_uf = uf[:n_aug] + torch.randn_like(uf[:n_aug]) * noise_scale.mean() * 0.1\\n                aug_refined = crm(aug_ba, None if obs_only else aug_uf, obs_feat[:n_aug])\\n                aug_loss = loss_fn(aug_refined, expert_flat[:n_aug])\\n                loss = loss + 0.5 * aug_loss\\n\\n            optimizer.zero_grad()\\n            loss.backward()\\n            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(crm.parameters(), 1.0)\\n            optimizer.step()\\n\\n            epoch_loss += loss.item()\\n            n_batches += 1\\n\\n            if (batch_idx + 1) % args.log_every == 0:\\n                print(f\\\"  epoch {epoch} batch {batch_idx+1}/{len(train_loader)} \\\"\\n                      f\\\"loss={loss.item():.6f}\\\")\\n\\n        scheduler.step()\\n        avg_train_loss = epoch_loss / max(n_batches, 1)\\n\\n        # ── Validate ──\\n        crm.eval()\\n        val_loss = 0.0\\n        n_val_batches = 0\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            for batch in val_loader:\\n                obs_feat = batch[\\\"obs_features\\\"].to(device)\\n                k_samp = batch[\\\"k_samples\\\"].to(device)\\n                expert = batch[\\\"expert_action\\\"].to(device)\\n                expert_flat = expert.reshape(expert.shape[0], -1)\\n\\n                uf, _mean = cgs.compute_features_batch(k_samp)\\n                if scalar_unc:\\n                    uf = _to_scalar_uf(uf)\\n                uf = uf.to(device)\\n                ba = _argmin_base_action(k_samp).to(device)\\n\\n                refined = crm(ba, None if obs_only else uf, obs_feat)\\n                val_loss += loss_fn(refined, expert_flat).item()\\n                n_val_batches += 1\\n\\n        avg_val_loss = val_loss / max(n_val_batches, 1)\\n\\n        print(f\\\"Epoch {epoch}/{args.epochs}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"train_loss={avg_train_loss:.6f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"val_loss={avg_val_loss:.6f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"lr={scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e}\\\")\\n\\n        metrics = {\\\"train_loss\\\": avg_train_loss, \\\"val_loss\\\": avg_val_loss}\\n        ckpt_mgr.save(crm, optimizer, epoch, metrics)\\n\\n        if avg_val_loss < best_val_loss:\\n            best_val_loss = avg_val_loss\\n            torch.save(crm.state_dict(),\\n                       Path(args.output_dir) / \\\"crm_best.pt\\\")\\n            print(f\\\"  → New best val_loss: {best_val_loss:.6f}\\\")\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n[train] Done. Best val_loss={best_val_loss:.6f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"[train] Checkpoints at {args.output_dir}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    args = parse_args()\\n    print(f\\\"[train_crm] args: {vars(args)}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"[train_crm] device: {'cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu'}\\\")\\n\\n    if args.precompute:\\n        precompute_features(args)\\n        return\\n\\n    if args.auto:\\n        needs_precompute = any(\\n            not (Path(args.cache_dir) / bm / \\\"manifest.json\\\").exists()\\n            for bm in args.benchmark\\n        )\\n        if needs_precompute:\\n            print(\\\"[auto] Cache not found for some benchmarks, precomputing...\\\")\\n            precompute_features(args)\\n\\n    train(args)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.4}}"

_input_mean ** 2).clamp(min=0).sqrt()\n    crm.set_normalization_stats(_input_mean, _input_std)\n    print(f\"[train] Norm stats set: mean [{_input_mean.min():.4f}, {_input_mean.max():.4f}], \"\n          f\"std [{_input_std.min():.4f}, {_input_std.max():.4f}]\")\n    del _stat_loader, _sum_x, _sum_x2, _input_mean, _input_std\n\n'''\n\ntarget = '    best_val_loss = float(\"inf\")'\nassert target in content, \"Could not find insertion point\"\ncontent = content.replace(target, norm_block + '    best_val_loss = float(\"inf\")')\n\nwith open('/root/acr-vla-conformal-refinement/src/train_crm.py', 'w') as f:\n    f.write(content)\n\nwith open('/root/acr-vla-conformal-refinement/src/train_crm.py', 'r') as f:\n    new = f.read()\nassert 'set_normalization_stats' in new\nassert 'Computing input normalization' in new\nprint(\"train_crm.py patched OK\")\nPYEOF\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python /tmp/patch_train.py",

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nConformal Refinement Module (CRM).\\n\\nInput: base_action (30D, argmin-selected) + uncertainty_features (40D) + obs_features (960D from VLM)\\nOutput: refined_action (30D) = base_action + clipped_delta\\n\\nArchitecture: 2 hidden layers, dim 256, ReLU, residual connection.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\n\\n\\nclass ConformalRefinementModule(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Learn to refine VLA action predictions using structured uncertainty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, action_dim: int = 30, uncertainty_dim: int = 40,\\n                 obs_dim: int = 960, hidden_dim: int = 256,\\n                 max_correction_norm: float = 1.42):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Args:\\n            action_dim: flattened action dim (H_eff * D = 5 * 6 = 30)\\n            uncertainty_dim: CGS output dim (30 + 5 + 5 = 40), 0 for obs_only\\n            obs_dim: VLM encoder frozen output dim (960)\\n            hidden_dim: hidden layer dim\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        super().__init__()\\n\\n        self.max_correction_norm = max_correction_norm\\n        self.uncertainty_dim = uncertainty_dim\\n        input_dim = action_dim + uncertainty_dim + obs_dim\\n\\n        self.register_buffer('input_mean', torch.zeros(input_dim))\\n        self.register_buffer('input_std', torch.ones(input_dim))\\n\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\n            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),\\n            nn.ReLU(),\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, hidden_dim),\\n            nn.ReLU(),\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, action_dim),\\n        )\\n\\n        # Residual: output = base_action + delta. Init last layer near zero.\\n        nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].weight)\\n        nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].bias)\\n\\n    def forward(self, base_action: torch.Tensor, uncertainty_features: torch.Tensor,\\n                obs_features: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Args:\\n            base_action: (B, 30) argmin-selected sample, flattened\\n            uncertainty_features: (B, 40) CGS output, or None for obs_only\\n            obs_features: (B, 960) VLM frozen obs features\\n        Returns:\\n            refined_action: (B, 30) corrected action\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if uncertainty_features is not None:\\n            x = torch.cat([base_action, uncertainty_features, obs_features], dim=-1)\\n        else:\\n            x = torch.cat([base_action, obs_features], dim=-1)\\n        x = (x - self.input_mean) / (self.input_std + 1e-8)\\n        delta = self.net(x)\\n        # L2 norm clipping to prevent catastrophic over-correction\\n        delta_norm = delta.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\\n        delta = delta * torch.clamp(self.max_correction_norm / (delta_norm + 1e-8), max=1.0)\\n        return base_action + delta\\n\\n    def set_normalization_stats(self, mean: \\\"torch.Tensor\\\", std: \\\"torch.Tensor\\\"):\\n        self.input_mean.copy_(mean)\\n        self.input_std.copy_(std)\\n\\n    def count_parameters(self) -> int:\\n        return sum(p.numel() for p in self.parameters() if p.requires_grad)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}

Dry-run 训练完成。

**结果汇总：**

| | Epoch 1 | Epoch 2 |
|---|---|---|
| train_loss | 0.024353 | 0.018892 |
| val_loss | 0.013509 | 0.010139 |

**修改内容：**

1. **`src/crm.py`** — 3 处修改：
   - `__init__()`: 添加 `register_buffer('input_mean', ...)` 和 `register_buffer('input_std', ...)`
   - `forward()`: 拼接后、进入 `self.net` 前加入 `x = (x - self.input_mean) / (self.input_std + 1e-8)`
   - 新增 `set_normalization_stats(mean, std)` 方法

2. **`src/train_crm.py`** — 1 处修改：
   - 在 `_to_scalar_uf` 定义后、训练循环前，遍历训练集计算 concatenated input 的 per-feature mean/std，调用 `crm.set_normalization_stats()`

**验证通过：**
- 单元测试：buffers 创建/设置/保存/加载/v2 兼容全部 OK
- Normalization stats：mean [-26.9, 5.5], std [0.004, 2.2]（证实 1500x scale 失衡）
- Dry-run 训练：loss 持续下降，2 epoch val_loss 从 0.0135 降到 0.0101
